# Complete factual tables — frozen tempo cohort

This supplement transcribes the official complete readout. It adds no biological interpretation, inference, or simulations.

Source JSON SHA256: `a1b5e84c61e9cc242af8a71bafe57bf852e233445e3e4e7476333aca252009af`. Status: `COMPLETE_SYNTHETIC_TEMPORAL_SEQUENCE_OUTCOME_SOURCE_ONLY_NOT_NATURAL_ECOLOGY`.

Design SHA256: `207a3a0ad3decf096c65f296bebeee69e7f80d5b644abb2318105e50474774d0`. Executable source identity SHA256: `9302c9740a1f5ac91cb1c1c39093f358c6fd745d4c5b768980e78d893aade80b`.

Denominators:1024 trajectories;16 independent synthetic profiles;4 nested demographic repeats;8 settings;64 trajectories per arm within each setting.

Setting column: mating timing / direct assurance cost / mutation rate. Primary probabilities are percentages; schedule differences and their intervals are percentage points (pp).

All intervals below are the source's two-sided descriptive percentile intervals:9999 profile resamples, seed48272026, tails .025/.975. NA denotes a source null; observed zero displays as0.

For secondary endpoints, mean_abrupt and mean_gradual use their separately eligible arm support. The paired-support means and paired contrast use only jointly eligible repeats, averaged first within profile. Support cells show eligible trajectories / eligible profiles. Counts/medians for sampled gradients are source profile averages, not pooled counts of sampled plants.

[Complete readout and per-endpoint profile_support arrays](https://github.com/zuizui0223/izu-core/blob/main/data/results/chapter2_sequence_abrupt_gradual_functional_loss_20261011.json.gz). All source secondary fields are included below; the16-profile arrays are retained in that linked readout.
